Standard approach: As[BK][BM] layout (transposed) to load A tile with coalesced reads and access as As[k][m]. Shared As[BK][BM+pad], Bs[BK][BN+pad]. Loading A from global A[row*N + k] where k contiguous within tile: threads load. Using As[k][m] means writing transposed (scatter) but reads for compute are contiguous in m. Common SGEMM uses As[BM][BK] and Bs[BK][BN] and each thread reads As[threadRow][k] etc. Let's do:\n\nAs[BM][BK+2] (128*(18)*8 = 18KB)\nBs[BK][BN+2] (16*130*8=16.6KB) total ~35KB, fine (max 48KB default dynamic? static shared per block max 48KB on sm_86 without opt-in). 35KB okay.\n\nLoading: 256 threads load BM*BK=2048 elements for As -> 8 per thread. Bs = BK*BN=2048 -> 8 per thread. Layout:\n- For As: linear index t = tid; for idx=t; idx<BM*BK; idx+=256: r=idx/BK, c=idx%BK. Global A[(rowStart+blockRow*BM+r)*N + k0+c]. This is coalesced across c within row (BK=16, so each row 16 consecutive doubles, threads consecutive idx -> within 16 then next row, mostly coalesced 128B chunks). Fine.\n- For Bs: idx: r=idx/BN, c=idx%BN. Global B[(k0+r)*N + blockCol*BN+c] coalesced in c (BN=128). Good.\n\nCompute: thread tid -> threadRow=tid/16 (0..15), threadCol=tid%16. Each computes rows baseRow = threadRow*TM (TM=8), cols baseCol=threadCol*TN (8). Accumulators acc[8][8]. For k in 0..BK-1: load aReg[8] = As[baseRow+i][k]; bReg[8] = Bs[k][baseCol+j]; acc[i][j] += aReg[i]*bReg[j]. 64 FMA. Compiler unrolls fully with #pragma unroll. Let's verify correctness.\n\nAlternatively use kernel with block 64x64, 8x8 thread tile, 64 threads? no.\n\nLet's settle templated kernel:\n\n```cpp\ntemplate<int BM, int BN, int BK, int TM, int TN, int THREADS>\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(THREADS)\nvoid matmulKernel(const double* __restrict__ A, const double* __restrict__ B,\n                  double* __restrict__ C, int M, int N) {\n  static_assert(THREADS == (BM/TM)*(BN/TN));\n  constexpr int TPR = BM/TM; // threads per row dimension\n  constexpr int TPC = BN/TN;\n  __shared__ double As[BK][BM + 1]; // transposed\n  __shared__ double Bs[BK][BN + 1];\n  const int tid = threadIdx.x;\n  const int threadRow = tid / TPC; // 0..TPR-1\n  const int threadCol = tid % TPC; // 0..TPC-1\n  const int rowBase = blockIdx.x * BM + threadRow * TM;\n  const int colBase = blockIdx.y * BN + threadCol * TN;\n  double acc[TM][TN] = {};\n\n  for (int k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += BK) {\n    // load As\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < BM*BK; idx += THREADS) {\n      int m = idx / BK;\n      int k = idx % BK;\n      int gm = blockIdx.x * BM + m;\n      int gk = k0 + k;\n      As[k][m] = (gm < M && gk < N) ? A[(size_t)gm * N + gk] : 0.0;\n    }\n    for (int idx = tid; idx < BK*BN; idx += THREADS) {\n      int k = idx / BN;\n      int n = idx % BN;\n      int gk = k0 + k;\n      int gn = blockIdx.y * BN + n;\n      Bs[k][n] = (gk < N && gn < N) ? B[(size_t)gk * N + gn] : 0.0;\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n    #pragma unroll 1? no\n    for (int k = 0; k < BK; ++k) {\n      double a[TM], b[TN];\n      #pragma unroll\n      for (int i=0;i<TM;++i) a[i] = As[k][threadRow*TM+i];\n      #pragma unroll\n      for (int j=0;j<TN;++j) b[j] = Bs[k][threadCol*TN+j];\n      #pragma unroll\n      for (int i=0;i<TM;++i)\n        #pragma unroll\n        for (int j=0;j<TN;++j)\n          acc[i][j] += a[i]*b[j];\n    }\n    __syncthreads();\n  }\n  // write\n  ...\n}\n```\n\nProblem: bounds check gk = k0+k where k from idx% BK; when k0+k >= N, A access guarded; also when k0 >= N loop doesn't execute.
